## User · 2026-08-03T17:40:23.227Z

OmniHarness direct-control instruction:
Do not implement, edit files, run mutating commands, or otherwise change the workspace unless the user's latest message explicitly asks you to implement, edit, modify, fix, create, delete, run, apply, or change something.
If the user's latest message asks how you would do something, asks for suggestions, asks for advice, asks for a plan, or says not to do anything, answer with analysis or a plan only.
If the user's intent is ambiguous, ask a clarifying question before making workspace changes.

User message:
Let's make some improvements to the mobile interface. 

One is, when tapping the name of the session on the top bar, that appears so small that we can't see what we're typing. On mobile we should get a little pop-up modal to change the session name. Also it feels like we are displaying the folder we're working on and maybe the repo or the branch I don't know, I see the same name like "omniharness" repeated at the top, and anyway on mobile I'm not sure that's super helpful to have there in the nav bar. We already have that in the input controls I think 

Beyond that we need, when we adjust the font size for the interface, the one that is tied to the UI elements, should actually make UI elements larger. Not just the font, but the actual buttons should just grow. This is especially important for phones and tablets. 

I'm on a phone right now and everything looks incredibly tiny and I have no way to change that. 

Speaking of which, I changed all font sizes, but the font size of the text in the input box doesn't seem to be affected by any of them

```text
19	import type { AgentSnapshot, ConversationSidebarTab, MessageRecord, RunRecord, SidebarGroup, SidebarRun, SupervisorInterventionRecord } from "@/interface/home/types";
20	import type { ManualCommitAction } from "@/lib/commit-workflow";
21	import type { ConversationWorkerRecord } from "@/lib/conversation-workers";
22	import type { WorkerTerminalProcess } from "@/lib/worker-terminal-processes";
23	import { t, useI18nSnapshot } from "@/lib/i18n";
24	import { ConversationSidebar } from "./ConversationSidebar";
25	import { RunWorkspaceBadge } from "./RunWorkspaceBadge";
26	import { ThemeModeToggle } from "./ThemeModeToggle";
27	
28	const SideWindow = lazy(
29	  () => import("./SideWindow").then((m) => ({ default: m.SideWindow })),
30	);
31	
32	const InteractiveTerminal = lazy(
33	  () => import("@/components/InteractiveTerminal").then((m) => ({ default: m.InteractiveTerminal })),
34	);
35	
36	interface HomeHeaderProps {
37	  mobileNavOpen: boolean;
38	  setMobileNavOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
39	  leftSidebarOpen: boolean;
40	  setLeftSidebarOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
41	  filteredProjects: SidebarGroup[];
42	  activeProjects: SidebarGroup[];
43	  conversationSidebarTab: ConversationSidebarTab;
44	  setConversationSidebarTab: (tab: ConversationSidebarTab) => void;
45	  isHydratingConversations: boolean;
46	  searchQuery: string;
47	  setSearchQuery: (value: string) => void;
48	  selectedRunId: string | null;
49	  messages: MessageRecord[];
50	  readMarkers: Record<string, string>;
51	  collapsedProjectPaths: Set<string>;
52	  visibleProjectSessionCounts: Record<string, number>;
53	  onProjectOpenChange: (projectPath: string, open: boolean) => void;
54	  onReorderProjects: (draggedPath: string, targetPath: string, placement: ProjectDropPlacement) => void;
55	  onCollapseAllProjects: (projectPaths: string[]) => void;
56	  onShowMoreProjectSessions: (projectPath: string) => void;
57	  setShowSettings: Dispatch<SetStateAction<boolean>>;
58	  openOnboarding: () => void;
59	  openFolderPicker: () => void;
60	  startNewPlan: () => void;
61	  beginConversationInProject: (projectPath: string) => void;
62	  autoCommitProject: (projectPath: string, action?: ManualCommitAction) => void;
63	  isAutoCommitProjectPending: boolean;
64	  handleRemoveProject: (pathToRemove: string) => void;
65	  selectRun: (runId: string) => void;
66	  renamingRunId: string | null;
67	  renameValue: string;
68	  renameSource: "sidebar" | "topbar" | null;
69	  setRenameValue: (value: string) => void;
70	  movingRunId: string | null;
71	  moveRunProjectPath: string;
72	  moveRunToProjectOptions: string[];
73	  setMoveRunProjectPath: (value: string) => void;
74	  startMovingRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
75	  confirmMoveRunToProject: () => void;
76	  cancelMovingRun: () => void;
77	  isMoveRunToProjectPending: boolean;
78	  startRenamingRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
79	  commitRenamingRun: (runId: string) => void;
80	  cancelRenamingRun: () => void;
81	  archiveRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
82	  deleteRun: (run: SidebarRun) => void;
83	  authEnabled: boolean;
84	  openPairDeviceDialog: () => void;
85	  logout: () => void;
86	  activeConversationCwd: string | null;
87	  selectedRun: RunRecord | null;
88	  isImplementationConversation: boolean;
89	  workspaceSideWindowAvailable: boolean;
90	  projectRoot: string | null;
91	  themeMode: "day" | "night";
92	  setThemeMode: Dispatch<SetStateAction<"day" | "night">>;
93	  rightSidebarOpen: boolean;
94	  setRightSidebarOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
95	  terminalPanelOpen: boolean;
96	  setTerminalPanelOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
97	  mobileWorkersOpen: boolean;
98	  setMobileWorkersOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
99	  mobileTerminalOpen: boolean;
100	  setMobileTerminalOpen: (open: boolean) => void;
101	  selectedRunWorkers: ConversationWorkerRecord[];
102	  conversationAgents: AgentSnapshot[];
103	  supervisorInterventions: SupervisorInterventionRecord[];
104	  onPrimaryCommit: () => void;
105	  onCommitNow: () => void;
106	  onCommitAndPushNow: () => void;
107	  autoCommitMilestonesEnabled: boolean;
108	  pushOnCommitEnabled: boolean;
109	  onAutoCommitMilestonesChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
110	  onPushOnCommitChange: (checked: boolean) => void;
111	  isAutoCommitChatPending: boolean;
112	  onStopWorker?: (workerId: string) => void;
113	  onStopTerminalProcess?: (workerId: string, terminalProcess: WorkerTerminalProcess) => void;
114	  onRespondElicitation?: (input: {
115	    workerId: string;
116	    requestId: number;
117	    action: "accept" | "decline" | "cancel";
118	    content?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | string[]>;
119	  }) => void;
120	  onRespondPermission?: (input: {
121	    workerId: string;
122	    requestId: number;
123	    decision: "approve" | "deny";
124	    optionId?: string;
125	  }) => void;
126	  onLoadWorkerHistory?: (workerId: string) => void;
127	  stoppingWorkerId?: string | null;
128	  stoppingTerminalProcess?: { workerId: string; terminalProcessId: string } | null;
129	  onForkSession: () => void;
130	  onForkSessionIntoWorktree: () => void;
131	  canForkSession: boolean;
132	  onReload: () => void;
133	  onOpenExternalSessions?: () => void;
134	}
135	
136	export function HomeHeader({
137	  mobileNavOpen,
138	  setMobileNavOpen,
139	  leftSidebarOpen,
140	  setLeftSidebarOpen,
141	  filteredProjects,
142	  activeProjects,
143	  conversationSidebarTab,
144	  setConversationSidebarTab,
145	  isHydratingConversations,
146	  searchQuery,
147	  setSearchQuery,
148	  selectedRunId,
149	  messages,
150	  readMarkers,
151	  collapsedProjectPaths,
152	  visibleProjectSessionCounts,
153	  onProjectOpenChange,
154	  onReorderProjects,
155	  onCollapseAllProjects,
156	  onShowMoreProjectSessions,
157	  setShowSettings,
158	  openOnboarding,
159	  openFolderPicker,
160	  startNewPlan,
161	  beginConversationInProject,
162	  autoCommitProject,
163	  isAutoCommitProjectPending,
164	  handleRemoveProject,
165	  selectRun,
166	  renamingRunId,
167	  renameValue,
168	  renameSource,
169	  setRenameValue,
170	  movingRunId,
171	  moveRunProjectPath,
172	  moveRunToProjectOptions,
173	  setMoveRunProjectPath,
174	  startMovingRun,
175	  confirmMoveRunToProject,
176	  cancelMovingRun,
177	  isMoveRunToProjectPending,
178	  startRenamingRun,
179	  commitRenamingRun,
180	  cancelRenamingRun,
181	  archiveRun,
182	  deleteRun,
183	  authEnabled,
184	  openPairDeviceDialog,
185	  logout,
186	  activeConversationCwd,
187	  selectedRun,
188	  isImplementationConversation,
189	  workspaceSideWindowAvailable,
190	  projectRoot,
191	  themeMode,
192	  setThemeMode,
193	  rightSidebarOpen,
194	  setRightSidebarOpen,
195	  terminalPanelOpen,
196	  setTerminalPanelOpen,
197	  mobileWorkersOpen,
198	  setMobileWorkersOpen,
199	  mobileTerminalOpen,
200	  setMobileTerminalOpen,
201	  selectedRunWorkers,
202	  conversationAgents,
203	  supervisorInterventions,
204	  onPrimaryCommit,
205	  onCommitNow,
206	  onCommitAndPushNow,
207	  autoCommitMilestonesEnabled,
208	  pushOnCommitEnabled,
209	  onAutoCommitMilestonesChange,
210	  onPushOnCommitChange,
211	  isAutoCommitChatPending,
212	  onStopWorker,
213	  onStopTerminalProcess,
214	  onRespondElicitation,
215	  onRespondPermission,
216	  onLoadWorkerHistory,
217	  stoppingWorkerId,
218	  stoppingTerminalProcess,
219	  onForkSession,
220	  onForkSessionIntoWorktree,
221	  canForkSession,
222	  onReload,
223	  onOpenExternalSessions,
224	}: HomeHeaderProps) {
225	  useI18nSnapshot();
226	  const conversationTitle = selectedRun?.title?.trim() || "New conversation";
227	  const titleLabel = selectedRun ? conversationTitle : "";
228	  const isEditingTitle = Boolean(selectedRun && renamingRunId === selectedRun.id && renameSource === "topbar");
229	  const rootFolderLabel = activeConversationCwd
230	    ? activeConversationCwd.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean).pop() || activeConversationCwd
231	    : "";
232	  const commitButtonLabel = pushOnCommitEnabled ? t("commit.menu.commitAndPushNow") : t("commit.menu.commitNow");
233	
234	  const beginTopBarTitleEdit = () => {
235	    if (!selectedRun) {
236	      return;
237	    }
238	
239	    startRenamingRun({
240	      id: selectedRun.id,
241	      title: conversationTitle,
242	      path: activeConversationCwd || selectedRun.projectPath || "",
243	      status: selectedRun.status,
244	      createdAt: selectedRun.createdAt,
245	    });
246	  };
247	
248	  const copyToClipboard = useCallback(async (value: string) => {
249	    if (!value) return;
250	    try {
251	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);
252	    } catch (error) {
253	      console.error("Copy failed:", error);
254	    }
255	  }, []);
256	
257	  const handleCopyWorkingDirectory = useCallback(() => {
258	    void copyToClipboard(activeConversationCwd || selectedRun?.projectPath || "");
259	  }, [copyToClipboard, activeConversationCwd, selectedRun?.projectPath]);
260	
261	  const handleCopySessionId = useCallback(() => {
262	    void copyToClipboard(selectedRunId || "");
263	  }, [copyToClipboard, selectedRunId]);
264	
265	  const handleCopyAsMarkdown = useCallback(() => {
266	    if (!selectedRun) return;
267	    const header = `# ${conversationTitle}\n`;
268	    const body = messages
269	      .filter((m) => m.runId === selectedRun.id && m.content?.trim())
270	      .map((m) => {
271	        const role = m.role === "user" ? "User" : m.role === "assistant" ? "Assistant" : m.role;
272	        return `## ${role}\n\n${m.content}`;
273	      })
274	      .join("\n\n");
275	    void copyToClipboard(`${header}\n${body}`);
276	  }, [copyToClipboard, selectedRun, conversationTitle, messages]);
277	
278	  const focusTitleInput = useCallback((node: HTMLInputElement | null) => {
279	    if (!node || !isEditingTitle) {
280	      return;
281	    }
282	
283	    node.focus();
284	    node.select();
285	  }, [isEditingTitle]);
286	
287	  return (
288	  <header className="flex h-14 shrink-0 items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-border/50 px-3 sm:px-4">
289	    <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 sm:gap-3">
290	      {!leftSidebarOpen ? (
291	        <Button
292	          variant="ghost"
293	          size="icon"
294	          className="hidden h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground transition-all duration-150 ease-out hover:text-foreground lg:inline-flex motion-reduce:transition-none"
295	          aria-label="Open conversations sidebar"
296	          title="Open conversations sidebar"
297	          onClick={() => setLeftSidebarOpen(true)}
298	        >
299	          <PanelLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
300	        </Button>
301	      ) : null}
302	      <Sheet open={mobileNavOpen} onOpenChange={setMobileNavOpen}>
303	        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8 lg:hidden" aria-label="Open navigation" onClick={() => setMobileNavOpen(true)}>
304	          <Menu className="h-4 w-4" />
305	        </Button>
306	        {mobileNavOpen ? (
307	          <SheetContent side="left" className="w-[min(22rem,calc(100vw-1rem))] p-0 lg:hidden" showCloseButton={false}>
308	            <SheetHeader className="border-b border-border/60">
309	              <SheetTitle className="flex items-center gap-2 text-left">
310	                <OmniHarnessMark className="h-8 w-8 p-1" />
311	                <span>{PRODUCT_NAME}</span>
312	              </SheetTitle>
313	            </SheetHeader>
314	            <ConversationSidebar
315	              runnerControlsMode="mobile"
316	              filteredProjects={filteredProjects as SidebarGroup[]}
317	              activeProjects={activeProjects as SidebarGroup[]}
318	              conversationSidebarTab={conversationSidebarTab}
319	              setConversationSidebarTab={setConversationSidebarTab}
320	              isHydratingConversations={isHydratingConversations}
321	              searchQuery={searchQuery}
322	              setSearchQuery={setSearchQuery}
323	              selectedRunId={selectedRunId}
324	              messages={messages}
325	              readMarkers={readMarkers}
326	              collapsedProjectPaths={collapsedProjectPaths}
327	              visibleProjectSessionCounts={visibleProjectSessionCounts}
328	              onProjectOpenChange={onProjectOpenChange}
329	              onReorderProjects={onReorderProjects}
330	              onCollapseAllProjects={onCollapseAllProjects}
331	              onShowMoreProjectSessions={onShowMoreProjectSessions}
332	              setShowSettings={setShowSettings}
333	              openOnboarding={openOnboarding}
334	              openFolderPicker={openFolderPicker}
335	              startNewPlan={startNewPlan}
336	              beginConversationInProject={beginConversationInProject}
337	              autoCommitProject={autoCommitProject}
338	              isAutoCommitProjectPending={isAutoCommitProjectPending}
339	              handleRemoveProject={handleRemoveProject}
340	              selectRun={selectRun}
341	              renamingRunId={renamingRunId}
342	              renameValue={renameValue}
343	              renameSource={renameSource}
344	              setRenameValue={setRenameValue}
345	              movingRunId={movingRunId}
346	              moveRunProjectPath={moveRunProjectPath}
347	              moveRunToProjectOptions={moveRunToProjectOptions}
348	              setMoveRunProjectPath={setMoveRunProjectPath}
349	              startMovingRun={startMovingRun}
350	              confirmMoveRunToProject={confirmMoveRunToProject}
351	              cancelMovingRun={cancelMovingRun}
352	              isMoveRunToProjectPending={isMoveRunToProjectPending}
353	              startRenamingRun={startRenamingRun}
354	              commitRenamingRun={commitRenamingRun}
355	              cancelRenamingRun={cancelRenamingRun}
356	              archiveRun={archiveRun}
357	              deleteRun={deleteRun}
358	              authEnabled={authEnabled}
359	              openPairDeviceDialog={openPairDeviceDialog}
360	              logout={logout}
361	              themeMode={themeMode}
362	              setThemeMode={setThemeMode}
363	              onOpenExternalSessions={onOpenExternalSessions}
364	            />
365	          </SheetContent>
366	        ) : null}
367	      </Sheet>
368	
369	      <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
370	        {titleLabel || rootFolderLabel ? (
371	          <div className="flex min-w-0 items-baseline gap-2">
372	            {titleLabel && selectedRun ? (
373	              isEditingTitle ? (
374	                <Input
375	                  ref={focusTitleInput}
376	                  aria-label="Edit conversation title"
377	                  value={renameValue}
378	                  onChange={(event) => setRenameValue(event.target.value)}
379	                  onKeyDown={(event) => {
380	                    event.stopPropagation();
381	                    if (event.key === "Enter") {
382	                      event.preventDefault();
383	                      commitRenamingRun(selectedRun.id);
384	                    } else if (event.key === "Escape") {
385	                      event.preventDefault();
386	                      cancelRenamingRun();
387	                    }
388	                  }}
389	                  className="h-8 w-[18rem] max-w-[calc(100vw-10rem)] rounded-md border-border/70 bg-background px-2 text-sm font-semibold sm:w-[26rem]"
390	                />
391	              ) : (
392	                <div className="group/title flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
393	                  <button
394	                    type="button"
395	                    aria-label="Conversation title"
396	                    title={`${titleLabel} — tap to rename`}
397	                    className="min-w-0 truncate rounded-sm px-1 text-left text-sm font-semibold text-foreground lg:hidden"
398	                    onClick={beginTopBarTitleEdit}
399	                  >
400	                    {titleLabel}
401	                  </button>
402	                  <span
403	                    aria-label="Conversation title"
404	                    className="hidden min-w-0 max-w-[26rem] truncate px-1 text-sm font-semibold text-foreground lg:block"
405	                    title={titleLabel}
406	                  >
407	                    {titleLabel}
408	                  </span>
409	                  <Button
410	                    type="button"
411	                    variant="ghost"
412	                    size="icon"
413	                    aria-label="Edit conversation title"
414	                    title="Edit conversation title"
415	                    className="pointer-events-none hidden h-7 w-7 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity hover:text-foreground focus-visible:pointer-events-auto focus-visible:opacity-100 group-hover/title:pointer-events-auto group-hover/title:opacity-100 lg:inline-flex"
416	                    onClick={beginTopBarTitleEdit}
417	                  >
418	                    <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
419	                  </Button>
420	                </div>
421	              )
422	            ) : null}
423	            {rootFolderLabel ? (
424	              <span
425	                aria-label="Root repository folder"
426	                className="max-w-[10rem] shrink-0 truncate font-mono text-[10px] text-muted-foreground"
427	                title={activeConversationCwd || rootFolderLabel}
428	              >
429	                {rootFolderLabel}
430	              </span>
431	            ) : null}
432	            <RunWorkspaceBadge run={selectedRun} fallbackPath={activeConversationCwd} />
433	            {selectedRun ? (
434	              <DropdownMenu>
435	                <DropdownMenuTrigger
436	                  render={(
437	                    <Button
438	                      variant="ghost"
439	                      size="icon"
440	                      className="h-7 w-7 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
441	                      aria-label={t("session.menu.label")}
442	                      title={t("session.menu.label")}
443	                    >
444	                      <MoreHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
445	                    </Button>
446	                  )}
447	                />
448	                <DropdownMenuContent align="start" className="w-60">
449	                  <DropdownMenuItem onClick={handleCopyWorkingDirectory} disabled={!activeConversationCwd && !selectedRun.projectPath}>
450	                    <span>{t("session.menu.copyWorkingDirectory")}</span>
451	                  </DropdownMenuItem>
452	                  <DropdownMenuItem onClick={handleCopySessionId} disabled={!selectedRunId}>
453	                    <span>{t("session.menu.copySessionId")}</span>
454	                  </DropdownMenuItem>
455	                  <DropdownMenuItem onClick={handleCopyAsMarkdown}>
456	                    <span>{t("session.menu.copyAsMarkdown")}</span>
457	                  </DropdownMenuItem>
458	                  <DropdownMenuSeparator />
459	                  <DropdownMenuItem onClick={onForkSession} disabled={!canForkSession}>
460	                    <GitBranch className="h-4 w-4" />
461	                    <span>{t("session.menu.forkSession")}</span>
462	                  </DropdownMenuItem>
463	                  <DropdownMenuItem onClick={onForkSessionIntoWorktree} disabled={!canForkSession}>
464	                    <FolderGit2 className="h-4 w-4" />
465	                    <span>{t("session.menu.forkIntoWorktree")}</span>
466	                  </DropdownMenuItem>
467	                  <DropdownMenuSeparator />
468	                  <DropdownMenuItem onClick={onReload}>
469	                    <RotateCw className="h-4 w-4" />
470	                    <span>{t("session.menu.reload")}</span>
471	                  </DropdownMenuItem>
472	                  <DropdownMenuSeparator className="sm:hidden" />
473	                  <DropdownMenuItem onClick={onCommitNow} disabled={isAutoCommitChatPending} className="sm:hidden">
474	                    <GitCommitHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
475	                    <span>{t("commit.menu.commitNow")}</span>
476	                  </DropdownMenuItem>
477	                  <DropdownMenuItem onClick={onCommitAndPushNow} disabled={isAutoCommitChatPending} className="sm:hidden">
478	                    <GitCommitHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
479	                    <span>{t("commit.menu.commitAndPushNow")}</span>
480	                  </DropdownMenuItem>
481	                </DropdownMenuContent>
482	              </DropdownMenu>
483	            ) : null}
484	          </div>
485	        ) : null}
486	        {selectedRun?.status === "cancelling" ? (
487	          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-amber-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-amber-700">
488	            Cancelling
489	          </span>
490	        ) : null}
491	      </div>
492	    </div>
493	
494	    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
495	      {selectedRunId ? (
496	        <ButtonGroup aria-label={t("commit.menu.label")} className="hidden sm:flex">
497	          <Button
498	            variant="ghost"
499	            size="icon-sm"
500	            className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
501	            aria-label={commitButtonLabel}
502	            title={commitButtonLabel}
503	            onClick={onPrimaryCommit}
504	            disabled={isAutoCommitChatPending}
505	          >
506	            <GitCommitHorizontal className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
507	          </Button>
508	          <DropdownMenu>
509	            <DropdownMenuTrigger
510	              render={(
511	                <Button
512	                  variant="ghost"
513	                  size="icon-sm"
514	                  className="h-8 w-7 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
515	                  aria-label={t("commit.menu.label")}
516	                  title={t("commit.menu.label")}
517	                  disabled={isAutoCommitChatPending}
518	                >
519	                  <ChevronDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
520	                </Button>
521	              )}
522	            />
523	            <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="w-64">
524	              <DropdownMenuItem
525	                onClick={(event) => {
526	                  event.preventDefault();
527	                  onAutoCommitMilestonesChange(!autoCommitMilestonesEnabled);
528	                }}
529	                className="gap-3"
530	              >
531	                <Switch
532	                  checked={autoCommitMilestonesEnabled}
533	                  onCheckedChange={onAutoCommitMilestonesChange}
534	                  onClick={(event) => {
535	                    event.stopPropagation();
536	                    onAutoCommitMilestonesChange(!autoCommitMilestonesEnabled);
537	                  }}
538	                  aria-label={t("commit.menu.autoCommitMilestones")}
539	                />
540	                <span>{t("commit.menu.autoCommitMilestones")}</span>
541	              </DropdownMenuItem>
542	              <DropdownMenuItem
543	                onClick={(event) => {
544	                  event.preventDefault();
545	                  onPushOnCommitChange(!pushOnCommitEnabled);
546	                }}
547	                className="gap-3"
548	              >
549	                <Switch
550	                  checked={pushOnCommitEnabled}
551	                  onCheckedChange={onPushOnCommitChange}
552	                  onClick={(event) => {
553	                    event.stopPropagation();
554	                    onPushOnCommitChange(!pushOnCommitEnabled);
555	                  }}
556	                  aria-label={t("commit.menu.alwaysPushOnCommit")}
557	                />
558	                <span>{t("commit.menu.alwaysPushOnCommit")}</span>
559	              </DropdownMenuItem>
560	              <DropdownMenuSeparator />
561	              <DropdownMenuItem onClick={onCommitNow} disabled={isAutoCommitChatPending}>
562	                <GitCommitHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
563	                <span>{t("commit.menu.commitNow")}</span>
564	              </DropdownMenuItem>
565	              <DropdownMenuItem onClick={onCommitAndPushNow} disabled={isAutoCommitChatPending}>
566	                <GitCommitHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
567	                <span>{t("commit.menu.commitAndPushNow")}</span>
568	              </DropdownMenuItem>
569	            </DropdownMenuContent>
570	          </DropdownMenu>
571	        </ButtonGroup>
572	      ) : null}
573	      <Button
574	        type="button"
575	        variant="ghost"
576	        size="icon"
577	        className="h-8 w-8 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground sm:hidden"
578	        aria-label={t("mainMenu.reportBug")}
579	        title={t("mainMenu.reportBug")}
580	        onClick={requestBugDropOpen}
581	      >
582	        <Bug className="h-4 w-4" />
583	      </Button>
584	      <Button
585	        type="button"
586	        variant="outline"
587	        size="sm"
588	        className="hidden sm:inline-flex h-8 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
589	        aria-label={t("mainMenu.reportBug")}
590	        title={t("mainMenu.reportBug")}
591	        onClick={requestBugDropOpen}
592	      >
593	        <Bug className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
594	        <span>{t("mainMenu.reportBug")}</span>
595	      </Button>
596	      <div className="hidden sm:block">
```

```text
13	interface TextSizeSliderProps {

15	  labelKey: string;
16	  descriptionKey: string;
17	  value: AppearanceTextSizeLevel;
18	  levels: typeof UI_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS;
19	  onChange: (value: AppearanceTextSizeLevel) => void;
20	}
21	
22	function TextSizeSlider({ id, labelKey, descriptionKey, value, levels, onChange }: TextSizeSliderProps) {
23	  const selectedIndex = Math.max(0, levels.findIndex((level) => level.value === value));
24	  const selectedLevel = levels[selectedIndex] ?? levels[0];
25	  const handleIndexChange = (index: number) => {
26	    const nextLevel = levels[index];
27	    if (nextLevel) {
28	      onChange(nextLevel.value);
29	    }
30	  };
31	
32	  return (
33	    <div className="grid gap-3 py-3 sm:grid-cols-[minmax(9rem,0.9fr)_minmax(14rem,1.35fr)] sm:items-center">
34	      <div className="min-w-0">
35	        <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-foreground" htmlFor={id}>
36	          {t(labelKey)}
37	        </label>
38	        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">{t(descriptionKey)}</p>
39	      </div>
40	      <div className="min-w-0">
41	        <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-end">
42	          <span className="rounded-md bg-muted px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold text-foreground">
43	            {t(selectedLevel.labelKey)}
44	          </span>
45	        </div>
46	        <div className="relative h-7">
47	          <input
48	            id={id}
49	            type="range"
50	            min={0}
51	            max={levels.length - 1}
52	            step={1}
53	            value={selectedIndex}
54	            aria-label={t(labelKey)}
55	            aria-valuetext={t(selectedLevel.labelKey)}
56	            onChange={(event) => handleIndexChange(Number(event.currentTarget.value))}
57	            onInput={(event) => handleIndexChange(Number(event.currentTarget.value))}
58	            className={cn(
59	              "absolute inset-x-0 top-1/2 z-10 h-2 -translate-y-1/2 cursor-pointer appearance-none rounded-full bg-transparent accent-primary",
60	              "[&::-moz-range-track]:h-1.5 [&::-moz-range-track]:rounded-full [&::-moz-range-track]:bg-muted",
61	              "[&::-moz-range-thumb]:h-4 [&::-moz-range-thumb]:w-4 [&::-moz-range-thumb]:rounded-full [&::-moz-range-thumb]:border-0 [&::-moz-range-thumb]:bg-primary",
62	              "[&::-webkit-slider-runnable-track]:h-1.5 [&::-webkit-slider-runnable-track]:rounded-full [&::-webkit-slider-runnable-track]:bg-muted",
63	              "[&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:relative [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:z-20 [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:-mt-[5px] [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:h-4 [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:w-4 [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:appearance-none [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:rounded-full [&::-webkit-slider-thumb]:bg-primary",
64	            )}
65	          />
66	          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 top-1/2 h-1.5 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full bg-muted" aria-hidden="true" />
67	          {levels.map((level, index) => (
68	            <button
69	              key={level.value}
70	              type="button"
71	              aria-label={`${t(labelKey)}: ${t(level.labelKey)}`}
72	              aria-pressed={level.value === value}
73	              onClick={() => onChange(level.value)}
74	              className={cn(
75	                "absolute top-1/2 z-30 h-5 w-5 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50",
76	              )}
77	              style={{ left: `${(index / (levels.length - 1)) * 100}%` }}
78	            >
79	              <span
80	                className={cn(
81	                  "mx-auto block h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full transition-colors",
82	                  level.value === value ? "bg-primary" : "bg-muted-foreground/35",
83	                )}
84	              />
85	            </button>
86	          ))}
87	        </div>
88	      </div>
89	    </div>
90	  );
91	}
92	
93	export function AppearanceSettingsPanel() {
94	  const { uiTextSize, conversationTextSize, terminalTextSize, alwaysExpandThoughts } = useManagerSnapshot(appearancePreferencesManager);
95	  useI18nSnapshot();
96	
97	  return (
98	    <div className="space-y-3">
99	      <div className="space-y-1">
100	        <div className="text-sm font-semibold">{t("settings.appearance.title")}</div>
101	      </div>
102	      <div className="grid gap-4">
103	        <TextSizeSlider
104	          id="OMNI_UI_TEXT_SIZE"
105	          labelKey="settings.appearance.uiFontSize"
106	          descriptionKey="settings.appearance.uiFontSizeDescription"
107	          value={uiTextSize}
108	          levels={UI_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS}
109	          onChange={(nextValue) => appearancePreferencesManager.setUiTextSize(nextValue)}
110	        />
111	        <TextSizeSlider
112	          id="OMNI_CONVERSATION_TEXT_SIZE"
113	          labelKey="settings.appearance.conversationFontSize"
114	          descriptionKey="settings.appearance.conversationFontSizeDescription"
115	          value={conversationTextSize}
116	          levels={CONVERSATION_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS}
117	          onChange={(nextValue) => appearancePreferencesManager.setConversationTextSize(nextValue)}
118	        />
119	        <TextSizeSlider
120	          id="OMNI_TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE"
121	          labelKey="settings.appearance.terminalFontSize"
122	          descriptionKey="settings.appearance.terminalFontSizeDescription"
123	          value={terminalTextSize}
124	          levels={TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS}
125	          onChange={(nextValue) => appearancePreferencesManager.setTerminalTextSize(nextValue)}
126	        />
127	        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 py-3">
128	          <Switch
129	            id="OMNI_ALWAYS_EXPAND_THOUGHTS"
130	            aria-label={t("settings.appearance.alwaysExpandThoughts")}
131	            checked={alwaysExpandThoughts}
132	            onCheckedChange={(checked) => appearancePreferencesManager.setAlwaysExpandThoughts(checked)}
133	          />
134	          <div className="min-w-0">
135	            <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-foreground" htmlFor="OMNI_ALWAYS_EXPAND_THOUGHTS">
136	              {t("settings.appearance.alwaysExpandThoughts")}
137	            </label>
138	            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs leading-5 text-muted-foreground">
139	              {t("settings.appearance.alwaysExpandThoughtsDescription")}
140	            </p>
141	          </div>
142	        </div>
143	      </div>
144	    </div>
145	  );
146	}
```

```text
4	export const UI_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY = "omni-ui-font-size";
5	export const CONVERSATION_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY = "omni-conversation-font-size";
6	export const TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY = "omni-terminal-text-size";
7	export const ALWAYS_EXPAND_THOUGHTS_STORAGE_KEY = "omni-always-expand-thoughts";
8	export const LEGACY_DIRECT_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY = "omni-direct-text-size";
9	
10	export const TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS = [
11	  { value: "tiny", labelKey: "settings.textSize.tiny", uiSize: 12, conversationSize: 12, conversationLineHeight: 20, terminalScale: 0.86 },
12	  { value: "small", labelKey: "settings.textSize.small", uiSize: 13, conversationSize: 13, conversationLineHeight: 22, terminalScale: 0.94 },
13	  { value: "default", labelKey: "settings.textSize.default", uiSize: 14, conversationSize: 14, conversationLineHeight: 24, terminalScale: 1 },
14	  { value: "large", labelKey: "settings.textSize.large", uiSize: 16, conversationSize: 16, conversationLineHeight: 28, terminalScale: 1.14 },
15	  { value: "huge", labelKey: "settings.textSize.huge", uiSize: 18, conversationSize: 18, conversationLineHeight: 32, terminalScale: 1.28 },
16	] as const;
17	
18	export const UI_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS = TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS;
19	export const CONVERSATION_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS = TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS;
20	export const TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS = TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS;
21	
22	export type AppearanceTextSizeLevel = (typeof TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS)[number]["value"];
23	export type UiTextSizeLevel = AppearanceTextSizeLevel;
24	export type ConversationTextSizeLevel = AppearanceTextSizeLevel;
25	export type TerminalTextSizeLevel = (typeof TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS)[number]["value"];
26	
27	const APPEARANCE_PREFERENCE_KEYS = ["uiTextSize", "conversationTextSize", "terminalTextSize", "alwaysExpandThoughts"] as const;
28	
29	type AppearancePreferenceKey = (typeof APPEARANCE_PREFERENCE_KEYS)[number];
30	
31	type AppearanceDraftValues = {
32	  uiTextSize: UiTextSizeLevel;
33	  conversationTextSize: ConversationTextSizeLevel;
34	  terminalTextSize: TerminalTextSizeLevel;
35	  alwaysExpandThoughts: boolean;
36	};
37	
38	type AppearancePreferencesState = AppearanceDraftValues & {
39	  savedUiTextSize: UiTextSizeLevel;
40	  savedConversationTextSize: ConversationTextSizeLevel;
41	  savedTerminalTextSize: TerminalTextSizeLevel;
42	  savedAlwaysExpandThoughts: boolean;
43	  dirtyKeys: Set<AppearancePreferenceKey>;
44	  hydrated: boolean;
45	};
46	
47	const DEFAULT_APPEARANCE_PREFERENCES: AppearancePreferencesState = {
48	  uiTextSize: "default",
49	  conversationTextSize: "default",
50	  terminalTextSize: "default",
51	  alwaysExpandThoughts: false,
52	  savedUiTextSize: "default",
53	  savedConversationTextSize: "default",
54	  savedTerminalTextSize: "default",
55	  savedAlwaysExpandThoughts: false,
56	  dirtyKeys: new Set(),
57	  hydrated: false,
58	};
59	
60	function canUseLocalStorage() {
61	  return typeof window !== "undefined" && typeof window.localStorage !== "undefined";
62	}
63	
64	function parseTextSize(value: string | null): AppearanceTextSizeLevel {
65	  if (TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS.some((level) => level.value === value)) {
66	    return value as AppearanceTextSizeLevel;
67	  }
68	
69	  switch (value) {
70	    case "compact":
71	      return "small";
72	    case "larger":
73	    case "largest":
74	      return "huge";
75	    default:
76	      return "default";
77	  }
78	}
79	
80	function writeLocalPreference(key: string, value: string) {
81	  if (!canUseLocalStorage()) {
82	    return;
83	  }
84	
85	  window.localStorage.setItem(key, value);
86	}
87	
88	function removeLocalPreference(key: string) {
89	  if (!canUseLocalStorage()) {
90	    return;
91	  }
92	
93	  window.localStorage.removeItem(key);
94	}
95	
96	function draftValues(state: AppearancePreferencesState): AppearanceDraftValues {
97	  return {
98	    uiTextSize: state.uiTextSize,
99	    conversationTextSize: state.conversationTextSize,
100	    terminalTextSize: state.terminalTextSize,
101	    alwaysExpandThoughts: state.alwaysExpandThoughts,
102	  };
103	}
104	
105	function savedValues(state: AppearancePreferencesState): AppearanceDraftValues {
106	  return {
107	    uiTextSize: state.savedUiTextSize,
108	    conversationTextSize: state.savedConversationTextSize,
109	    terminalTextSize: state.savedTerminalTextSize,
110	    alwaysExpandThoughts: state.savedAlwaysExpandThoughts,
111	  };
112	}
113	
114	function getAppearanceDirtyKeys(draft: AppearanceDraftValues, saved: AppearanceDraftValues) {
115	  const dirtyKeys = new Set<AppearancePreferenceKey>();
116	
117	  for (const key of APPEARANCE_PREFERENCE_KEYS) {
118	    if (draft[key] !== saved[key]) {
119	      dirtyKeys.add(key);
120	    }
121	  }
122	
123	  return dirtyKeys;
124	}
125	
126	function toScaledPx(baseSize: number, scale: number) {
127	  return `${Math.round(baseSize * scale * 10) / 10}px`;
128	}
129	
130	function textSizeLevel(level: AppearanceTextSizeLevel) {
131	  return TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS.find((candidate) => candidate.value === level) ?? TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS[2];
132	}
133	
134	function boostedPx(size: number, boostVariable: string) {
135	  return `calc(${size}px + var(${boostVariable}, 0px))`;
136	}
137	
138	export function getUiTextSizeStyle(level: UiTextSizeLevel): CSSProperties {
139	  const textSize = textSizeLevel(level);
140	
141	  return {
142	    "--omni-ui-font-size": boostedPx(textSize.uiSize, "--omni-mobile-ui-font-boost"),
143	    "--omni-ui-xxs-size": boostedPx(Math.max(10, textSize.uiSize - 4), "--omni-mobile-ui-font-boost"),
144	    "--omni-ui-xs-size": boostedPx(Math.max(11, textSize.uiSize - 2), "--omni-mobile-ui-font-boost"),
145	    "--omni-ui-sm-size": boostedPx(textSize.uiSize, "--omni-mobile-ui-font-boost"),
146	    "--omni-ui-base-size": boostedPx(textSize.uiSize + 2, "--omni-mobile-ui-font-boost"),
147	    "--omni-ui-control-xs-size": boostedPx(textSize.uiSize + 12, "--omni-mobile-ui-control-boost"),
148	    "--omni-ui-control-sm-size": boostedPx(textSize.uiSize + 15, "--omni-mobile-ui-control-boost"),
149	    "--omni-ui-control-md-size": boostedPx(textSize.uiSize + 18, "--omni-mobile-ui-control-boost"),
150	    "--omni-ui-control-lg-size": boostedPx(textSize.uiSize + 24, "--omni-mobile-ui-control-boost"),
151	    "--omni-ui-icon-sm-size": boostedPx(textSize.uiSize + 1, "--omni-mobile-ui-icon-boost"),
152	    "--omni-ui-icon-md-size": boostedPx(textSize.uiSize + 2, "--omni-mobile-ui-icon-boost"),
153	  } as CSSProperties;
154	}
155	
156	export function getConversationTextSizeStyle(level: ConversationTextSizeLevel): CSSProperties {
157	  const textSize = textSizeLevel(level);
158	
159	  return {
160	    "--omni-conversation-font-size": boostedPx(textSize.conversationSize, "--omni-mobile-conversation-font-boost"),
161	    "--omni-conversation-xs-size": boostedPx(Math.max(12, textSize.conversationSize - 2), "--omni-mobile-conversation-font-boost"),
162	    "--omni-conversation-sm-size": boostedPx(textSize.conversationSize, "--omni-mobile-conversation-font-boost"),
163	    "--omni-conversation-line-height": boostedPx(textSize.conversationLineHeight, "--omni-mobile-conversation-line-boost"),
164	  } as CSSProperties;
165	}
166	
167	export function getAppearanceTextSizeStyle(uiTextSize: UiTextSizeLevel, conversationTextSize: ConversationTextSizeLevel): CSSProperties {
168	  return {
169	    ...getUiTextSizeStyle(uiTextSize),
170	    ...getConversationTextSizeStyle(conversationTextSize),
171	  } as CSSProperties;
172	}
173	
174	const TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES = {
175	  message: 14,
176	  thought: 13,
177	  thoughtLabel: 13,
178	  toolLabel: 13,
179	  toolTitle: 12,
180	  pane: 11,
181	  paneLabel: 10,
182	  badge: 10,
183	  permissionTitle: 13,
184	  permissionText: 12,
185	};
186	
187	export function getTerminalTextSizeStyle(level: TerminalTextSizeLevel): CSSProperties {
188	  const scale = textSizeLevel(level).terminalScale;
189	
190	  return {
191	    "--terminal-message-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.message, scale),
192	    "--terminal-thought-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.thought, scale),
193	    "--terminal-thought-label-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.thoughtLabel, scale),
194	    "--terminal-tool-label-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.toolLabel, scale),
195	    "--terminal-tool-title-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.toolTitle, scale),
196	    "--terminal-pane-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.pane, scale),
197	    "--terminal-pane-label-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.paneLabel, scale),
198	    "--terminal-badge-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.badge, scale),
199	    "--terminal-permission-title-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.permissionTitle, scale),
200	    "--terminal-permission-text-size": toScaledPx(TERMINAL_BASE_FONT_SIZES.permissionText, scale),
201	  } as CSSProperties;
202	}
203	
204	export function getConversationTerminalTextSizeStyle(level: ConversationTextSizeLevel): CSSProperties {
205	  return getTerminalTextSizeStyle(level);
206	}
207	
208	export class AppearancePreferencesManager extends StateManager<AppearancePreferencesState> {
209	  constructor() {
210	    super(DEFAULT_APPEARANCE_PREFERENCES);
211	  }
212	
213	  hydrateFromLocalStorage() {
214	    if (!canUseLocalStorage()) {
215	      this.setKey("hydrated", true);
216	      return;
217	    }
218	
219	    const uiTextSize = parseTextSize(window.localStorage.getItem(UI_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY));
220	    const conversationTextSize = parseTextSize(
221	      window.localStorage.getItem(CONVERSATION_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY)
222	        ?? window.localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_DIRECT_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY),
223	    );
224	    const terminalTextSize = parseTextSize(window.localStorage.getItem(TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY));
225	    const alwaysExpandThoughts = window.localStorage.getItem(ALWAYS_EXPAND_THOUGHTS_STORAGE_KEY) === "true";
226	
227	    this.patch({
228	      uiTextSize,
229	      conversationTextSize,
230	      terminalTextSize,
231	      alwaysExpandThoughts,
232	      savedUiTextSize: uiTextSize,
233	      savedConversationTextSize: conversationTextSize,
234	      savedTerminalTextSize: terminalTextSize,
235	      savedAlwaysExpandThoughts: alwaysExpandThoughts,
236	      dirtyKeys: new Set(),
237	      hydrated: true,
238	    });
239	  }
240	
241	  private setDraft(values: Partial<AppearanceDraftValues>) {
242	    this.patch((current) => {
243	      const draft = { ...draftValues(current), ...values };
244	
245	      return {
246	        ...draft,
247	        dirtyKeys: getAppearanceDirtyKeys(draft, savedValues(current)),
248	        hydrated: true,
249	      };
250	    });
251	  }
252	
253	  setUiTextSize(uiTextSize: UiTextSizeLevel) {
254	    this.setDraft({ uiTextSize });
255	  }
256	
257	  setConversationTextSize(conversationTextSize: ConversationTextSizeLevel) {
258	    this.setDraft({ conversationTextSize });
259	  }
260	
261	  setTerminalTextSize(terminalTextSize: TerminalTextSizeLevel) {
262	    this.setDraft({ terminalTextSize });
263	  }
264	
265	  setAlwaysExpandThoughts(alwaysExpandThoughts: boolean) {
266	    this.setDraft({ alwaysExpandThoughts });
267	  }
268	
269	  saveDraft() {
270	    const { uiTextSize, conversationTextSize, terminalTextSize, alwaysExpandThoughts } = this.getSnapshot();
271	    writeLocalPreference(UI_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY, uiTextSize);
272	    writeLocalPreference(CONVERSATION_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY, conversationTextSize);
273	    writeLocalPreference(TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY, terminalTextSize);
274	    writeLocalPreference(ALWAYS_EXPAND_THOUGHTS_STORAGE_KEY, alwaysExpandThoughts ? "true" : "false");
275	    this.patch({
276	      savedUiTextSize: uiTextSize,
277	      savedConversationTextSize: conversationTextSize,
278	      savedTerminalTextSize: terminalTextSize,
279	      savedAlwaysExpandThoughts: alwaysExpandThoughts,
280	      dirtyKeys: new Set(),
281	      hydrated: true,
282	    });
283	  }
284	
285	  discardDraft() {
286	    this.patch((current) => ({
287	      ...savedValues(current),
288	      dirtyKeys: new Set(),
289	      hydrated: true,
290	    }));
291	  }
292	
293	  reset() {
294	    removeLocalPreference(UI_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY);
295	    removeLocalPreference(CONVERSATION_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY);
296	    removeLocalPreference(TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY);
297	    removeLocalPreference(ALWAYS_EXPAND_THOUGHTS_STORAGE_KEY);
298	    removeLocalPreference(LEGACY_DIRECT_TEXT_SIZE_STORAGE_KEY);
299	    this.patch({
300	      ...DEFAULT_APPEARANCE_PREFERENCES,
301	      dirtyKeys: new Set(),
302	    });
303	  }
304	}
305	
306	export const appearancePreferencesManager = new AppearancePreferencesManager();
307
```

```text
1	import { StateManager, type StateListener } from "@/lib/state-manager"
2	import { useManagerSnapshot } from "@/lib/use-manager-snapshot"
3	
4	const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768
5	
6	class ViewportManager extends StateManager<{ isMobile: boolean }> {
7	  private mediaQuery: MediaQueryList | null = null
8	  private listenerCount = 0
9	
10	  constructor() {
11	    super({ isMobile: false })
12	  }
13	
14	  private readonly handleChange = () => {
15	    this.setKey("isMobile", window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT)
16	  }
17	
18	  override subscribe(listener: StateListener) {
19	    if (typeof window !== "undefined" && this.listenerCount === 0) {
20	      this.mediaQuery = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`)
21	      this.mediaQuery.addEventListener("change", this.handleChange)
22	      this.handleChange()
23	    }
24	    this.listenerCount += 1
25	    const unsubscribe = super.subscribe(listener)
26	    return () => {
27	      unsubscribe()
28	      this.listenerCount -= 1
29	      if (this.listenerCount === 0) {
30	        this.mediaQuery?.removeEventListener("change", this.handleChange)
31	        this.mediaQuery = null
32	      }
33	    }
34	  }
35	}
36	
37	export const viewportManager = new ViewportManager()
38	
39	export function useIsMobile() {
40	  return useManagerSnapshot(viewportManager).isMobile
41	}
42
```

```text
38	function isLoopbackOrigin(origin: string) {
39	  try {
40	    const hostname = new URL(origin).hostname;
41	    return hostname === "localhost" || hostname === "127.0.0.1" || hostname === "::1";
42	  } catch {
43	    return false;
44	  }
45	}
46	
47	function resolvePairingOrigin(publicOrigin: string | null | undefined) {
48	  const configuredOrigin = publicOrigin?.trim().replace(/\/+$/, "");
49	  if (configuredOrigin && !isLoopbackOrigin(configuredOrigin)) {
50	    return configuredOrigin;
51	  }
52	
53	  const currentOrigin = window.location.origin;
54	  if (!isLoopbackOrigin(currentOrigin)) {
55	    return currentOrigin;
56	  }
57	
58	  return null;
59	}
60	
61	export function PairDeviceDialog({
62	  open,
63	  onOpenChange,
64	  selectedRunId = null,
65	  publicOrigin = null,
66	  availabilityError = null,
67	}: PairDeviceDialogProps) {
68	  const runtimeApis = useRuntimeAPIs();
69	  const {
70	    pairing,
71	    pairingStatus,
72	    qrDataUrl,
73	    isLoading,
74	    isActivating,
75	    error,
76	    copyNotice,
77	    nowMs,
78	  } = useManagerSnapshot(pairDeviceManager) as PairingState<PairCreateResponse, PairStatusResponse["pairing"]["status"]>;
79	
80	  const expiresInSeconds = useMemo(() => {
81	    if (!pairing?.expiresAt) {
82	      return null;
83	    }
84	    return Math.max(0, Math.ceil((new Date(pairing.expiresAt).getTime() - nowMs) / 1000));
85	  }, [nowMs, pairing?.expiresAt]);
86	
87	  const createPairing = useCallback(async () => {
88	    const pairingOrigin = resolvePairingOrigin(publicOrigin);
89	    if (!pairingOrigin) {
90	      pairDeviceManager.patch({
91	        isLoading: false,
92	        isActivating: false,
93	        error: "Phone pairing needs a public or LAN URL. Set OMNIHARNESS_PUBLIC_ORIGIN or open OmniHarness through its tunnel/LAN address.",
94	        copyNotice: null,
95	        pairing: null,
96	        pairingStatus: null,
97	        qrDataUrl: null,
98	        nowMs: Date.now(),
99	      });
100	      return;
101	    }
102	
103	    const draft = createLocalPairingDraft({
104	      origin: pairingOrigin,
105	      targetRunId: selectedRunId,
106	    });
107	
108	    pairDeviceManager.patch({
109	      isLoading: true,
110	      error: null,
111	      copyNotice: null,
112	      pairingStatus: null,
113	      isActivating: false,
114	      nowMs: Date.now(),
115	    });
116	
117	    try {
118	      const nextQrDataUrl = await QRCode.toDataURL(draft.pairUrl, {
119	        margin: 1,
120	        width: 320,
121	        color: {
122	          dark: "#18211d",
123	          light: "#fbfdf9",
124	        },
125	      });
126	
127	      pairDeviceManager.patch({
128	        pairing: {
129	          pairingId: draft.pairingId,
130	          pairUrl: draft.pairUrl,
131	          expiresAt: draft.expiresAt,
132	        },
133	        pairingStatus: "pending",
134	        qrDataUrl: nextQrDataUrl,
135	        isLoading: false,
136	        isActivating: true,
137	      });
138	
139	      const data = await runtimeApis.auth.createPair({
140	          targetRunId: selectedRunId,
141	          pairToken: draft.pairToken,
142	      }) as PairCreateResponse;
143	
144	      pairDeviceManager.patch((current) => {
145	        const currentPairing = current.pairing as PairCreateResponse | null;
146	        return currentPairing && data.pairingId === draft.pairingId
147	          ? {
148	            pairing: data,
149	            pairingStatus: "pending",
150	            qrDataUrl: nextQrDataUrl,
151	            isActivating: false,
152	          }
153	          : {};
154	      });
155	    } catch (pairError) {
156	      const message = pairError instanceof Error ? pairError.message : String(pairError);
157	      pairDeviceManager.patchIfCurrentPairing(draft.pairingId, {
158	        error: message,
159	        isLoading: false,
160	        isActivating: false,
161	      });
162	    }
163	  }, [publicOrigin, runtimeApis.auth, selectedRunId]);
164	
165	  const refreshPairing = useCallback(() => {
166	    void createPairing();
167	  }, [createPairing]);
168	
169	  useEffect(() => {
170	    if (!open) {
171	      return;
172	    }
173	
174	    if (availabilityError) {
175	      return;
176	    }
177	
178	    if (!pairing && !isLoading) {
179	      void createPairing();
180	    }
181	  }, [availabilityError, createPairing, open, pairing, isLoading]);
182	
183	  useEffect(() => {
184	    if (!open || !pairing?.expiresAt || pairingStatus !== "pending") {
185	      return;
186	    }
187	
188	    const interval = window.setInterval(() => pairDeviceManager.setKey("nowMs", Date.now()), 1000);
189	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
190	  }, [open, pairing?.expiresAt, pairingStatus]);
191	
192	  useEffect(() => {
193	    if (!open || !pairing?.pairingId || pairingStatus !== "pending" || isActivating || error) {
194	      return;
195	    }
196	
197	    const interval = window.setInterval(async () => {
198	      const pollRequestId = pairDeviceManager.beginStatusPoll(pairing.pairingId);
199	      try {
200	        const data = await runtimeApis.auth.getPair({
201	          id: pairing.pairingId,
202	        }) as PairStatusResponse;
203	
204	        pairDeviceManager.patchIfCurrentStatusPoll(pairing.pairingId, pollRequestId, {
205	          pairingStatus: data.pairing.status,
206	        });
207	      } catch (pollError) {
208	        pairDeviceManager.patchIfCurrentStatusPoll(pairing.pairingId, pollRequestId, {
209	          error: pollError instanceof Error ? pollError.message : String(pollError),
210	        });
211	      }
212	    }, 2000);
213	
214	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
215	  }, [error, isActivating, open, pairing?.pairingId, pairingStatus, runtimeApis.auth]);
216	
217	  useEffect(() => {
218	    if (!open) {
219	      pairDeviceManager.reset();
220	    }
221	  }, [open]);
222

264	                <div className="max-w-xs rounded-lg border border-destructive/20 bg-destructive/5 p-4 text-sm text-destructive">

266	                </div>
267	              </div>

271	                  <LoaderCircle className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />

274	              </div>

278	                  <img src={qrDataUrl} alt="Pair device QR code" className="aspect-square w-full max-w-[280px]" />
279	                </div>
```

```text
145	const TOOL_OUTPUT_COLLAPSED_MAX_HEIGHT = "calc(var(--terminal-pane-size) * 4.65 + 1rem)";
146	const TOOL_OUTPUT_EXPANDED_MAX_HEIGHT = "min(72vh, 42rem)";
147	const TERMINAL_REVEAL_CLASS = "grid transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity,transform] duration-300 ease-[cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)] motion-reduce:transition-none";
148	const TERMINAL_REVEAL_OPEN_CLASS = "grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100 translate-y-0";
149	const TERMINAL_REVEAL_CLOSED_CLASS = "grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0 -translate-y-1 pointer-events-none";
150	const TERMINAL_TOOL_STATUSES = new Set(["completed", "done", "failed", "error", "cancelled"]);
151	const TERMINAL_TOP_THRESHOLD_PX = 4;
152	export { TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS };
153	export type TerminalZoomLevel = TerminalTextSizeLevel;
154	export const getTerminalZoomStyle = getTerminalTextSizeStyle;
155	
156	export function getTerminalActivityVersion(activity: TerminalActivityItem[]) {
157	  return activity.map((item) => {
158	    switch (item.kind) {
159	      case "pending_assistant":
160	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.status}`;
161	      case "thinking":
162	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.inProgress}:${item.thoughts.join("\n").length}`;
163	      case "tool":
164	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.status}:${item.title.length}:${item.inputPane?.text.length ?? 0}:${item.outputPane?.text.length ?? 0}`;
165	      case "tool_group":
166	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.status}:${item.tools.length}:${item.tools.map((tool) => `${tool.id}:${tool.status}:${tool.title.length}:${tool.inputPane?.text.length ?? 0}:${tool.outputPane?.text.length ?? 0}`).join(",")}`;
167	      case "permission":
168	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.status}:${item.title.length}:${item.text.length}:${item.detail?.length ?? 0}`;
169	      case "message":
170	      case "user_message":
171	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.timestamp}:${item.text.length}`;
172	      case "work_summary":
173	        return `${item.id}:${item.kind}:${item.durationMs}:${item.inProgress}:${item.items.length}`;
174	    }
175	  }).join("|");
176	}
177	
178	export function shouldTerminalConnectorExtend(
179	  activityKind: TerminalActivityKind,
180	  adjacentActivityKind: TerminalActivityKind | undefined,
181	) {
182	  return activityKind !== "user_message"
183	    && adjacentActivityKind !== undefined
184	    && adjacentActivityKind !== "user_message";
185	}
186	
187	export function TerminalTextSizeControl({ className }: { className?: string }) {
188	  const { terminalTextSize } = useManagerSnapshot(appearancePreferencesManager);
189	  useI18nSnapshot();
190	
191	  return (
192	    <DropdownMenu>
193	      <DropdownMenuTrigger
194	        className={cn(
195	          "inline-flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-background/95 text-muted-foreground shadow-sm transition-colors hover:bg-muted hover:text-foreground focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/45 dark:border-white/10 dark:bg-[#15181d]/95 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-[#1d2128] dark:hover:text-zinc-100 dark:focus-visible:ring-cyan-300/45",
196	          className,
197	        )}
198	        aria-label={t("settings.appearance.terminalFontSize")}
199	        title={t("settings.appearance.terminalFontSize")}
200	      >
```

```text
1	import { FolderGit2, GitBranch } from "lucide-react";
2	import type { RunRecord } from "@/interface/home/types";
3	import type { GitWorkspaceRunSnapshot } from "@/lib/git-workspace";
4	import { t } from "@/lib/i18n";
5	import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
6	
7	interface RunWorkspaceBadgeProps {
8	  run: RunRecord | null;
9	  fallbackPath?: string | null;
10	  className?: string;
11	}
12	
13	function parseRunWorkspaceSnapshot(value: string | null | undefined): GitWorkspaceRunSnapshot | null {
14	  if (!value) {
15	    return null;
16	  }
17	  try {
18	    const parsed = JSON.parse(value) as Partial<GitWorkspaceRunSnapshot>;
19	    if (parsed.target?.checkoutPath) {
20	      return parsed as GitWorkspaceRunSnapshot;
21	    }
22	  } catch {
23	    return null;
24	  }
25	  return null;
26	}
27	
28	function lastPathSegment(value: string | null | undefined) {
29	  return value?.split(/[\\/]/).filter(Boolean).pop() || value || "";
30	}
31	
32	export function RunWorkspaceBadge({ run, fallbackPath, className }: RunWorkspaceBadgeProps) {
33	  const snapshot = parseRunWorkspaceSnapshot(run?.gitWorkspaceJson);
34	  const checkoutPath = snapshot?.target.checkoutPath ?? fallbackPath ?? run?.projectPath ?? null;
35	  if (!run || !checkoutPath) {
36	    return null;
37	  }
38	
39	  const branchLabel = snapshot?.branchName ?? snapshot?.detachedLabel ?? null;
40	  const label = branchLabel
41	    ? branchLabel
42	    : lastPathSegment(checkoutPath) || t("git.workspace.runBadge.fallback");
43	  const title = branchLabel
44	    ? t("git.workspace.runBadge.titleWithBranch", { branch: branchLabel, path: checkoutPath })
45	    : t("git.workspace.runBadge.titleWithPath", { path: checkoutPath });
46	  const isWorktree = snapshot?.target.kind === "worktree";
47	
48	  return (
49	    <span
50	      className={cn(
51	        "inline-flex max-w-[14rem] shrink items-center gap-1 truncate rounded-full border border-border/70 bg-muted/35 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-muted-foreground",
52	        className,
53	      )}
54	      title={title}
55	      aria-label={title}
56	    >
57	      {isWorktree ? <FolderGit2 className="h-3 w-3" /> : <GitBranch className="h-3 w-3" />}
58	      <span className="truncate">{label}</span>
59	    </span>
60	  );
61	}
62
```

```text
1	"use client";
2	
3	import type React from "react";
4	import { memo, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
5	import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
6	import { ConversationComposer } from "@/components/home/ConversationComposer";
7	import type { PendingChatAttachment } from "@/lib/chat-attachments";
8	import type { ProjectFileReference } from "@/lib/project-file-links";
9	import { getActiveMentionQuery, replaceActiveMention } from "@/lib/mentions";
10	import { shallowEqualRecord, useManagerSelector } from "@/lib/use-manager-snapshot";
11	import {
12	  isManualStopCommand,
13	  resolveBusyComposerBehavior,
14	  resolveBusyMessageActionForSubmitAction,
15	  type BusyMessageAction,
16	} from "./busy-message-behavior";
17	import { homeUiSetters, homeUiStateManager, type HomeUiState } from "./HomeUiStateManager";
18	import type {
19	  ComposerMode,
20	  ComposerWorkerOption,
21	  ConversationModeOption,
22	  ProjectFilesResponse,
23	  QueuedConversationMessageRecord,
24	  WorkerModelOption,
25	} from "./types";
26	import { useRuntimeAPIs } from "@/runtime-api/provider";
27	
28	type ComposerDraftState = Pick<HomeUiState, "command" | "commandCursor" | "mentionIndex" | "attachments">;
29	
30	function selectComposerDraftState(s: HomeUiState): ComposerDraftState {
31	  return {
32	    command: s.command,
33	    commandCursor: s.commandCursor,
34	    mentionIndex: s.mentionIndex,
35	    attachments: s.attachments,
36	  };
37	}
38	
39	export interface ComposerContainerProps {
40	  className: string;
41	  commandInputRef: React.RefObject<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>;
42	  selectedRunId: string | null;
43	  selectedConversationMode: ComposerMode;
44	  setSelectedConversationMode: (value: ConversationModeOption) => void;
45	  currentProjectScope: string | null;
46	  projectFiles: string[];
47	  projectFilesIsFetched: boolean;
48	  onOpenProjectFile: (f: string | ProjectFileReference) => void;
49	  themeMode: "day" | "night";
50	  shouldLockDirectWorker: boolean;
51	  lockedDirectWorkerLabel: string;
52	  selectedCliAgent: ComposerWorkerOption;
53	  setSelectedCliAgent: (value: ComposerWorkerOption) => void;
54	  composerWorkerOptions: Array<{ value: ComposerWorkerOption; label: string }>;
55	  selectedWorkerAccountId: string;
56	  setSelectedWorkerAccountId: (value: string) => void;
57	  composerAccountOptions: Array<{ value: string; label: string }>;
58	  selectedModel: string;
59	  setSelectedModel: (value: string) => void;
60	  activeWorkerModelOptions: WorkerModelOption[];
61	  selectedEffort: string;
62	  setSelectedEffort: (value: string) => void;
63	  isComposerSubmitting: boolean;
64	  isStopConversationPending: boolean;
65	  isConversationStoppable: boolean;
66	  hasBusyConversation: boolean;
67	  busyMessageAction: BusyMessageAction;
68	  queuedMessages: QueuedConversationMessageRecord[];
69	  cancellingQueuedMessageIds: Set<string>;
70	  interruptingQueuedMessageIds: Set<string>;
71	  onEditQueuedMessage: (message: QueuedConversationMessageRecord) => void;
72	  onInterruptQueuedMessage: (messageId: string) => void;
73	  onCancelQueuedMessage: (messageId: string) => void;
74	  onInterruptConversation: (draft: { content: string; attachments: PendingChatAttachment[] } | null) => void;
75	  onSendConversationMessage: (content: string, attachments: PendingChatAttachment[], busyAction?: BusyMessageAction) => void;
76	  onRunCommand: (content: string, attachments: PendingChatAttachment[]) => void;
77	  onStopConversation: () => void;
78	}
79	
80	// Isolates high-churn draft subscriptions so keystrokes don't re-render HomeApp
81	function ComposerContainerInner({
82	  className,
83	  commandInputRef,
84	  selectedRunId,
85	  selectedConversationMode,
86	  setSelectedConversationMode,
87	  currentProjectScope,
88	  projectFiles,
89	  projectFilesIsFetched,
90	  onOpenProjectFile,
91	  themeMode,
92	  shouldLockDirectWorker,
93	  lockedDirectWorkerLabel,
94	  selectedCliAgent,
95	  setSelectedCliAgent,
96	  composerWorkerOptions,
97	  selectedWorkerAccountId,
98	  setSelectedWorkerAccountId,
99	  composerAccountOptions,
100	  selectedModel,
101	  setSelectedModel,
102	  activeWorkerModelOptions,
103	  selectedEffort,
104	  setSelectedEffort,
105	  isComposerSubmitting,
106	  isStopConversationPending,
107	  isConversationStoppable,
108	  hasBusyConversation,
109	  busyMessageAction,
110	  queuedMessages,
111	  cancellingQueuedMessageIds,
112	  interruptingQueuedMessageIds,
113	  onEditQueuedMessage,
114	  onInterruptQueuedMessage,
115	  onCancelQueuedMessage,
116	  onInterruptConversation,
117	  onSendConversationMessage,
118	  onRunCommand,
119	  onStopConversation,
120	}: ComposerContainerProps) {
121	  const runtimeApis = useRuntimeAPIs();
122	  const { setCommandCursor, setMentionIndex, setComposerDraft, addAttachmentFiles, addPastedImages, removeAttachment } = homeUiSetters;
123	
124	  const { command, commandCursor, mentionIndex, attachments } = useManagerSelector(
125	    homeUiStateManager,
126	    selectComposerDraftState,
127	    shallowEqualRecord,
128	  );
129	
130	  const activeMention = getActiveMentionQuery(command, commandCursor);
131	  const projectFilesQuery = useQuery<ProjectFilesResponse>({
132	    queryKey: ["project-files", currentProjectScope],
133	    queryFn: () => runtimeApis.files.list({
134	      root: currentProjectScope || "",
135	    }) as Promise<ProjectFilesResponse>,
136	    enabled: Boolean(activeMention && currentProjectScope),
137	    staleTime: 60_000,
138	  });
139	  const availableProjectFiles = projectFilesQuery.data?.files ?? projectFiles;
140	  const projectFilesReady = projectFilesIsFetched || projectFilesQuery.isFetched;
141	
142	  const filteredProjectFiles = useMemo(() => {
143	    if (!activeMention) return [];
144	    const needle = activeMention.query.toLowerCase();
145	    return availableProjectFiles
146	      .filter((f) => needle.length === 0 || f.toLowerCase().includes(needle))
147	      .slice(0, 12);
148	  }, [activeMention, availableProjectFiles]);
149	
150	  const showMentionPicker = Boolean(
151	    activeMention && currentProjectScope && (filteredProjectFiles.length > 0 || projectFilesReady),
152	  );
153	
154	  const composerBehavior = resolveBusyComposerBehavior({
155	    hasBusyConversation,
156	    isConversationStoppable,
157	    hasContent: Boolean(command.trim() || attachments.length > 0),
158	    busyMessageAction,
159	    forceSteer: selectedConversationMode === "implementation",
160	  });
161	
162	  useEffect(() => {
163	    setMentionIndex(0);
164	  }, [activeMention?.query, currentProjectScope, setMentionIndex]);
165	
166	  const applyMention = (filePath: string) => {
167	    if (!activeMention) return;
168	    const nextValue = replaceActiveMention(command, activeMention, filePath);
169	    const nextCursor = activeMention.start + filePath.length + 2;
170	    setComposerDraft({ command: nextValue, commandCursor: nextCursor });
171	    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
172	      commandInputRef.current?.focus();
173	      commandInputRef.current?.setSelectionRange(nextCursor, nextCursor);
174	    });
175	  };
176	
177	  const handleSubmit = (event: React.FormEvent) => {
178	    event.preventDefault();
179	    if (composerBehavior.submitAction === "stop") { onStopConversation(); return; }
180	    if (!command.trim() && attachments.length === 0) return;
181	    if (selectedRunId && attachments.length === 0 && isManualStopCommand(command)) {
182	      homeUiSetters.setComposerDraft({ command: "", commandCursor: 0 });
183	      onStopConversation();
184	      return;
185	    }
186	    if (selectedRunId) {
187	      onSendConversationMessage(command, attachments, resolveBusyMessageActionForSubmitAction(composerBehavior.submitAction));
188	      return;
189	    }
190	    onRunCommand(command, attachments);
191	  };
192	
193	  return (
194	    <ConversationComposer
195	      className={className}
196	      command={command}
197	      setCommandCursor={setCommandCursor}
198	      setComposerDraft={setComposerDraft}
199	      commandInputRef={commandInputRef}
200	      handleSubmit={handleSubmit}
201	      selectedRunId={selectedRunId}
202	      selectedConversationMode={selectedConversationMode}
203	      setSelectedConversationMode={setSelectedConversationMode}
204	      showMentionPicker={showMentionPicker}
205	      currentProjectScope={currentProjectScope}
206	      workspaceProjectPath={currentProjectScope}
207	      filteredProjectFiles={filteredProjectFiles}
208	      mentionIndex={mentionIndex}
209	      setMentionIndex={setMentionIndex}
210	      applyMention={applyMention}
211	      onOpenProjectFile={onOpenProjectFile}
212	      themeMode={themeMode}
213	      attachments={attachments}
214	      handleRemoveAttachment={removeAttachment}
215	      onAddAttachmentFiles={addAttachmentFiles}
216	      onAddPastedImages={addPastedImages}
217	      shouldLockDirectWorker={shouldLockDirectWorker}
218	      lockedDirectWorkerLabel={lockedDirectWorkerLabel}
219	      selectedCliAgent={selectedCliAgent}
220	      setSelectedCliAgent={setSelectedCliAgent}
221	      composerWorkerOptions={composerWorkerOptions}
222	      selectedWorkerAccountId={selectedWorkerAccountId}
223	      setSelectedWorkerAccountId={setSelectedWorkerAccountId}
224	      composerAccountOptions={composerAccountOptions}
225	      selectedModel={selectedModel}
226	      setSelectedModel={setSelectedModel}
227	      activeWorkerModelOptions={activeWorkerModelOptions}
228	      selectedEffort={selectedEffort}
229	      setSelectedEffort={setSelectedEffort}
230	      isComposerSubmitting={isComposerSubmitting}
231	      isStopConversationPending={isStopConversationPending}
232	      isConversationStoppable={isConversationStoppable}
233	      composerBehavior={composerBehavior}
234	      queuedMessages={queuedMessages}
235	      cancellingQueuedMessageIds={cancellingQueuedMessageIds}
236	      interruptingQueuedMessageIds={interruptingQueuedMessageIds}
237	      onEditQueuedMessage={onEditQueuedMessage}
238	      onInterruptQueuedMessage={onInterruptQueuedMessage}
239	      onCancelQueuedMessage={onCancelQueuedMessage}
240	      onInterruptComposer={() => {
241	        const hasDraft = Boolean(command.trim() || attachments.length > 0);
242	        onInterruptConversation(hasDraft ? { content: command, attachments } : null);
243	      }}
244	      onSendConversationMessage={(content, busyAction) => onSendConversationMessage(content, attachments, busyAction)}
245	      onRunCommand={(content) => onRunCommand(content, attachments)}
246	      onStopConversation={onStopConversation}
247	    />
248	  );
249	}
250	
251	export const ComposerContainer = memo(ComposerContainerInner);
252	ComposerContainer.displayName = "ComposerContainer";
253
```

```text
144	  const isSubmitButtonDisabled = isStopButtonVisible

146	    : isComposerSubmitting || (!trimmedCommand && !hasAttachments);
147	  const alternateSubmitShortcutLabel = getComposerSubmitShortcutLabel(isAppleComposerShortcutPlatform());
148	  const sendButtonAriaLabel = t(composerBehavior.ariaLabelKey);
149	  const sendButtonTitle = composerBehavior.submitAction === "send_queue"
150	    ? t("conversation.composer.sendButton.queueTitle", { shortcut: alternateSubmitShortcutLabel })
151	    : composerBehavior.submitAction === "send_steer" && composerBehavior.allowAlternateBusyAction
152	      ? t("conversation.composer.sendButton.steerTitle", { shortcut: alternateSubmitShortcutLabel })

162	  const mobileSettingsSummary = `${selectedHarnessLabel} · ${selectedAccountLabel} · ${selectedModelLabel}`;
```

```text
74	  return `${parent}/${repoName}-${slugBranchName(branchName).replace(/\//g, "-")}`;
75	}
```

```text
126	function eventCreatedAtMs(event: Pick<ExecutionEventRecord, "createdAt">) {
127	  const time = new Date(event.createdAt).getTime();
128	  return Number.isFinite(time) ? time : null;
129	}
130	
131	function hasRecoverErrorClearingEvent(events: ExecutionEventRecord[], submittedAt: number | undefined) {
132	  const submittedAtMs = typeof submittedAt === "number" && Number.isFinite(submittedAt) && submittedAt > 0
133	    ? submittedAt
134	    : null;
135	  return events.some((event) => {
136	    if (!RECOVER_ERROR_CLEARING_EVENT_TYPES.has(event.eventType)) {
137	      return false;
138	    }
139	    if (submittedAtMs === null) {
140	      return true;
141	    }
142	    const eventTime = eventCreatedAtMs(event);
143	    return eventTime !== null && eventTime >= submittedAtMs;
144	  });
145	}
146	
147	/**
148	 * React Query holds the last mutation error until the mutation is reset, so an
149	 * error raised while viewing run A is still `mutation.error` after the user has
150	 * moved to run B. Conversation-scoped failures belong to their own
151	 * conversation only: mismatch → suppress. Mutations with no run in their
152	 * variables (settings, project-wide commits) stay app-global.
153	 */
154	function scopedMutationError(
155	  error: unknown,
156	  variablesRunId: string | null | undefined,
157	  selectedRunId: string | null,
158	) {
159	  if (!error) {
160	    return null;
161	  }
162	  return !variablesRunId || variablesRunId === selectedRunId ? error : null;
163	}
164	
165	function shouldShowRecoverRunError(args: {
166	  error: unknown;
167	  variablesRunId: string | null | undefined;
168	  selectedRunId: string | null;
169	  selectedRun: RunRecord | null;
170	  selectedRunExecutionEvents: ExecutionEventRecord[];
171	  submittedAt: number | undefined;
172	}) {
173	  if (!args.error || !args.variablesRunId || args.variablesRunId !== args.selectedRunId) {
174	    return false;
175	  }
176	  if (args.selectedRun && args.selectedRun.status !== "failed" && args.selectedRun.status !== "needs_recovery") {
177	    return false;
178	  }
179	  if (hasRecoverErrorClearingEvent(args.selectedRunExecutionEvents, args.submittedAt)) {
180	    return false;
181	  }
182	  return true;
183	}
184

198	    ? bootstrap?.route

200	  const initialSnapshotScope = initialRoute?.selectedRunId ?? null;
```

```text
1	/* Layer 1: Motion primitives */
2	
3	@keyframes fxFadeIn {
4	  from { opacity: 0; }
5	  to   { opacity: 1; }
6	}
7	@keyframes fxFadeUp {
8	  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(var(--fx-y, 8px)); }
9	  to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
10	}
11	@keyframes fxFadeDown {
12	  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(calc(-1 * var(--fx-y, 8px))); }
13	  to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
14	}
15	@keyframes fxFadeRight {
16	  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateX(calc(-1 * var(--fx-x, 8px))); }
17	  to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateX(0); }
18	}
19	@keyframes fxFadeLeft {
20	  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateX(var(--fx-x, 8px)); }
21	  to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateX(0); }
22	}
23	@keyframes fxPopIn {
24	  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(var(--fx-y, 0px)) scale(var(--fx-scale-from, 0.94)); }
25	  to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0) scale(1); }
26	}
27	@keyframes fxSlideUp {
28	  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(var(--fx-y, 20px)) scale(var(--fx-scale-from, 1)); max-height: 0; }
29	  to   { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0) scale(1); max-height: var(--fx-max-h, 800px); }
30	}
31	@keyframes fxExpand {
32	  from { opacity: 0; max-height: 0; }
33	  to   { opacity: 1; max-height: var(--fx-max-h, 800px); }
34	}
35	@keyframes fxZoomOut {
36	  from { transform: scale(var(--fx-zoom-from, 1.5)); }
37	  to   { transform: scale(1); }
38	}
39	@keyframes fxZoomIn {
40	  from { transform: scale(1); }
41	  to   { transform: scale(var(--fx-zoom-to, 1.5)); }
42	}
43	@keyframes fxPulse {
44	  0%, 100% { transform: scale(1); box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 var(--fx-pulse-color, transparent); }
45	  50%      { transform: scale(var(--fx-pulse-scale, 0.9)); box-shadow: 0 0 0 6px var(--fx-pulse-color, currentColor); }
46	}
47	
48	/* Layer 2 — Composition triggers (utility classes) */
49	
50	/* PLAY ONCE — fires when its owning scene becomes active. */
51	[data-walkthrough-scene][data-active="true"] .fxPlay {
52	  animation:
53	    var(--fx-effect, fxFadeUp)
54	    var(--fx-duration, 380ms)
55	    var(--fx-easing, cubic-bezier(0.34, 1.4, 0.6, 1))
56	    var(--fx-at, 0s)
57	    var(--fx-fill, both);
58	}
59	
60	/* STAGGER — delay = base + index * stagger. */
61	[data-walkthrough-scene][data-active="true"] .fxStagger {
62	  animation:
63	    var(--fx-effect, fxFadeUp)
64	    var(--fx-duration, 320ms)
65	    var(--fx-easing, ease-out)
66	    var(--fx-fill, both);
67	  animation-delay: calc(var(--fx-at, 0s) + var(--row-i, 0) * var(--fx-stagger, 0.18s));
68	}
69	
70	/* WORDS — constant per-word rate = total duration / word count. */
71	[data-walkthrough-scene][data-active="true"] .fxWord {
72	  animation:
73	    var(--fx-effect, fxFadeIn)
74	    var(--fx-word-duration, 140ms)
75	    var(--fx-easing, ease-out)
76	    var(--fx-fill, both);
77	  animation-delay: calc(
78	    var(--fx-at, 0s) +
79	    var(--word-i, 0) * calc(var(--fx-duration, 2.6s) / var(--word-count, 1))
80	  );
81	}
```

````text
### 1. Appearance Settings Panel File

The panel defines three independent font size controls with five levels each (tiny, small, default, large, huge):

- **Conversation Font Size** (Line 111-118): Controls chat message text
  - Slider ID: `OMNI_CONVERSATION_TEXT_SIZE`
  - Levels: `CONVERSATION_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS`
  
- **Terminal Font Size** (Line 119-126): Controls terminal/output text
  - Slider ID: `OMNI_TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE`
  - Levels: `TERMINAL_TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS`

- **Always Expand Thoughts** (Line 127-142): Boolean toggle for thought expansion

**Value Ranges (AppearancePreferencesManager.ts, Lines 10-16):**
All three use the same `TEXT_SIZE_LEVELS` array:
```
tiny:      uiSize: 12,  conversationSize: 12,  terminalScale: 0.86
small:     uiSize: 13,  conversationSize: 13,  terminalScale: 0.94
default:   uiSize: 14,  conversationSize: 14,  terminalScale: 1.0
large:     uiSize: 16,  conversationSize: 16,  terminalScale: 1.14
huge:      uiSize: 18,  conversationSize: 18,  terminalScale: 1.28
```
````
